However, given that VLN agent requires a sequence of steps, standard calibration in conformal prediction fails to provide coverage guarantee it promises over a dependent, variable-length VLN episode. To this end, we propose Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP), which rescales a nonconformity score by the policy's residual confidence and calibrates one maximum score per episode. Under exchangeable calibration and test episodes, this construction covers the ground truth at every step with probability at least $1 - α$, while allowing dependence among steps within an episode. Across four VLN policies and three nonconformity scores on R2R and REVERIE dataset, ENCP meets all reported empirical step-coverage targets on the seen-to-unseen evaluation. These results demonstrate that ENCP can provide model-agnostic uncertainty estimates, which might be useful for determining when a VLN agent should defer to a more capable predictor, including human assistance.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [Scheduling Jobs with Multiple Operational Modes and Tail Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16001)
*Bo Chen,Jelmer Pier van der Gaast,Xiandong Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究受可编程材料（如液晶显示器）生产启发的调度问题：每个工件需选择加工模式（决定机器加工时间与随后的"tail"成熟时长），并确定加工顺序。作者对完工时间、完成时间同步性、加权完工时间等目标进行了计算复杂性分类，发现双模式即导致多数问题难解，模式数增大则难度显著上升，但存在若干可高效求解的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源于可编程材料（如先进液晶显示器）的生产过程：产品最终质量在"无资源占用"的成熟期（tail）后才达成，而此期间机器可继续加工其他工件。加工模式的选择会同时影响机器加工时间与tail时长，因而需要联合决策模式与排序，以优化多种生产目标。

Method: 建立调度模型：每个工件可在多种模式中选择，模式决定机器加工时间与后续tail时长；任务为同时选择每个工件的最优模式并确定加工顺序。针对总完工时间、完成时间同步性（最早与最晚完工产品之差）、总加权完工时间等目标，进行系统的计算复杂性分析，并与单模式传统版本及多模式情形对比。

Result: 单模式（每工件仅一种模式）的传统版本可用标准规则简单求解；引入仅两种模式后，大多数目标下的最优解求解变为计算困难；模式数很大时复杂性显著增加。同时识别出仍可高效求解的特例：加工顺序已确定时，或目标为最小化平均完工时间时。

Conclusion: 为涉及强制冷却或成熟阶段的复杂制造与化学过程调度提供了理论清晰性与实用优化策略：模式选择与排序的联合决策会显著提升问题难度，但在特定结构或目标下仍可高效求解。

Abstract: This study explores a scheduling challenge inspired by the production of programmable materials, such as advanced liquid crystal displays. In these systems, the final quality of a product is reached only after a resource-free maturation period, known as a "tail", during which the machine is available for processing other jobs. Each job can be executed in one of several operational modes, with each mode determining a specific combination of machine processing time and subsequent tail duration. The primary task is to simultaneously choose the best mode for every job and determine their processing order. We analyze this model across several key performance goals, including the total time required to finish all jobs, the synchronization of completion times (the gap between the earliest and latest finished products), and the total weighted completion time. Our findings provide a detailed classification of the computational complexity of these problems. We demonstrate that while traditional versions with only one mode per job are simple to solve using standard rules, the introduction of just two modes makes finding optimal solutions for most of these goals computationally difficult. When the number of available modes is large, the complexity increases significantly. However, we also identify specific scenarios that remain efficiently solvable, such as when the processing order is already determined or when the goal is to minimize the average completion time. These results offer theoretical clarity and practical strategies for optimizing complex manufacturing and chemical processes involving forced cooling or maturation stages.

</details>


### [247] [SETH-based Lower Bound for Dynamic Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16303)
*Konrad Majewski,Michał Pilipczuk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究动态图退化度近似值维护问题，证明在SETH假设下不存在$(2-\varepsilon)$近似的快速数据结构，从而补全Christiansen与Rotenberg的上界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图退化度的近似维护已有上界结果（Christiansen & Rotenberg, ICALP 2022），可在poly(d_max, log n)最坏更新时间内维护介于d与2d+3之间的整数值。作者希望探究该上界是否可进一步改进，特别是能否达到优于2的近似比，因此需要一个匹配的条件性下界。

Method: 条件性下界证明，基于强指数时间假设（SETH）进行归约，分析在任意ε, δ>0、k∈N及函数f: N→N下，维护(2−ε)近似退化度所需的数据结构初始化时间f(d_max)·n^k与摊还更新时间f(d_max)·n^{1−δ}的不可行性。

Result: 证明除非SETH失败，否则对任意ε, δ>0、k∈N及函数f，不存在能以初始化时间f(d_max)·n^k和摊还更新时间f(d_max)·n^{1−δ}维护图G退化度(2−ε)近似的数据结构。

Conclusion: 该下界与已知上界（近似比2、poly(d_max, log n)更新时间）基本吻合，表明近似比2是当前框架下的自然极限；任何将近似比降到2以下的方法都需突破SETH或重新设计更新时间的多项式结构。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the problem of maintaining an approximate value of degeneracy of a given dynamic $n$-vertex graph $G$ updated by edge insertions and deletions. From the work of Christiansen and Rotenberg [ICALP 2022], it follows that one can design a dynamic data structure for this problem with worst-case update time $\text{poly}(d_{\mathrm{max}}, \log n)$ that maintains an integer between $d$ and $2d+3$ where $d$ is the degeneracy of $G$, under the assumption that $d$ never exceeds $d_{\mathrm{max}}$. We complement their result by providing a conditional lower bound: we prove that, unless SETH fails, for any $\varepsilon, δ> 0$, $k \in \mathbb{N}$, and function $f\colon \mathbb{N}\to \mathbb{N}$, there is no data structure which maintains a $(2-\varepsilon)$-approximation of the degeneracy of $G$ with initialization time $f(d_{\mathrm{max}})\cdot n^k$ and amortized update time $f(d_{\mathrm{max}})\cdot n^{1-δ}$.

</details>


### [248] [A deterministic $(2 + \varepsilon)$-approximation for directed feedback vertex sets in tournaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16723)
*Ebrahim Ghorbani,Matthias Mnich*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文首次给出锦标赛中定向反馈顶点集问题（DFVS in tournaments）的确定性多项式时间 (2+ε)-近似算法，几乎达到已知的 2 近似下界，并推广至拟传递有向图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锦标赛中 DFVS 是 Vertex Cover-hard 问题，在唯一游戏猜想（UGC）下无法有多项式时间的 (2-ε)-近似，因此 2 是近似屏障。过去 28 年中一系列工作不断缩小近似因子（5/2 → 7/3 → 9/4），但确定性多项式时间的 2-近似仍为悬而未决问题（Lokshtanov 等, SODA 2020）。

Method: 提出确定性多项式时间算法，实现锦标赛中 DFVS 的 (2+ε)-近似，并将方法推广到更广泛的拟传递有向图（quasi-transitive digraphs）类。

Result: 给出首个锦标赛中 DFVS 的确定性多项式时间 (2+ε)-近似（对任意 ε>0），优于此前所有结果（5/2、7/3、9/4 近似），几乎解决了 Lokshtanov 等提出的确定性 2-近似公开问题。

Conclusion: 该结果几乎完全刻画了锦标赛中 DFVS 的多项式时间近似可达性，并推广到拟传递有向图，逼近 UGC 下的 2 近似下界。

Abstract: We nearly settle the polynomial-time approximability of the Directed Feedback Vertex Set problem in tournaments. This problem is Vertex Cover-hard, and thus cannot have a $(2 - \varepsilon)$-approximation for any $\varepsilon > 0$ in polynomial time assuming the Unique Games Conjecture. In the past 28 years, several works have attempted to attain this approximability barrier of 2, and have designed algorithms with smaller and smaller approximation factors. This includes a $5/2$-approximation by Cai, Deng and Zang (FOCS 1998, SICOMP 2001); a $7/3$-approximation by Mnich, Vassilevska Williams and V{é}gh (ESA 2016), another $7/3$-approximation by Aprile, Drescher, Fiorini and Huynh (DAM 2023), and a $9/4$-approximation by Ghorbani and Mnich (ICALP 2026). Our main result improves upon all of those works: we give the first deterministic polynomial-time $(2+\varepsilon)$-approximation for Directed Feedback Vertex Set in tournaments, for all $\varepsilon > 0$. We thereby almost answer an open question by Lokshtanov, Misra, Mukherjee, Panolan, Philip and Saurabh (SODA 2020) who asked for a deterministic 2-approximation in polynomial time. Furthermore, we extend our result to the broader class of quasi-transitive digraphs

</details>


### [249] [Improved Approximation for Unsplittable CVRP via a Greedy Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16910)
*Daniel Ebert,Leonard Weismantel*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对度量不可分割容量车辆路径问题（CVRP）的多项式时间 3.159-近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进度量不可分割容量车辆路径问题的近似比，在现有相对贪婪算法与LP舍入算法基础上进一步优化。

Method: 基于Traub（2025）的相对贪婪算法，核心创新为平均贪婪算法（Average Greedy Algorithm），可同时控制路径成本与高需求客户的覆盖；再结合路径划分变体与匹配算法。

Result: 得到多项式时间的 3.159-近似算法。

Conclusion: 平均贪婪算法带来的额外控制增强了对后续贪婪选择成本的平均化论证，结合路径划分与匹配技术可得到更优的近似保证。

Abstract: We devise a polynomial-time $3.159$-approximation algorithm for the metric unsplittable Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem. We build on the Relative Greedy Algorithm suggested by Traub (2025), which can be considered as a variant of the LP rounding algorithm of Friggstad, Mousavi, Rahgoshay, and Salavatipour (2025). Our main ingredient is the Average Greedy Algorithm, a new algorithm that controls both tour costs and the coverage of clients with high demand. This additional control enables a sharper averaging argument for the cost of subsequent greedy choices. Similarly to Zhao and Xiao (2026), combining the Average Greedy with variants of tour partitioning and a matching algorithm yields the final approximation guarantee.

</details>


### [250] [Improved Regular Expression Matching with Simple Backreferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16914)
*Philip Bille,Inge Li Gørtz,Rikke Schjeldrup Jessen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对含单个捕获组与k个反向引用的正则表达式（rewb）匹配问题，提出O(n²m)时间、O(nm)空间的算法，较此前O(kn²m²)时间和O(n²m²)空间的结果有显著改进，并扩展到有序单嵌套rewb。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rewb匹配问题在一般情况下是NP完全问题，但在单捕获组这一子类上存在多项式算法。此前最优算法（Uezato CPM 2026）为O(kn²m²)时间和O(n²m²)空间；k=1时可做到O(n²m²)时间和O(n+m²)空间。同时存在基于正交向量假设的条件下界，说明无法在O(n^{2-ε} poly(m))时间内求解。本文旨在缩小时间与空间上的差距，逼近该下界。

Method: 针对仅含单个捕获组和k个反向引用的rewb类，设计新的匹配算法，将时间复杂度降至O(n²m)、空间复杂度降至O(nm)。核心思想是通过对捕获组匹配位置的更紧凑枚举与动态规划状态压缩，避免之前算法中因引用数k和模式长度m带来的额外因子。进一步将方法推广至有序且单嵌套（ordered, single-nested）的rewb类。

Result: 新算法达到O(n²m)时间和O(nm)空间，相较Uezato算法分别改进了km倍（时间）和m倍（空间），相较Nogami–Terauchi的O(n²m²)/O(n+m²)结果在时间上改进m倍，并改善前者空间界nm倍。同时展示了算法可扩展至更一般的有序单嵌套rewb类。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了单捕获组rewb匹配的算法复杂度，在时间与空间上均取得实质性改进，并逼近已知条件下界；方法的可扩展性表明其适用于更广的一类反向引用正则表达式。未来方向可能包括进一步降低空间或处理更多捕获组的情形。

Abstract: A regular expression with backreferences (rewb) specifies a set of strings formed by characters combined with concatenation, union, star operators, and backreferences. A backreference consists of a capturing group $(\cdot)_i$ and a reference $\backslash i$. The substring matched by the reference must match the substring matched by the corresponding capturing group. Given a rewb $R$ and a string $Q$, the rewb matching problem is to decide whether $Q$ is one of the strings specified by $R$.
  In full generality, rewb matching is NP-complete, but efficient solutions exist for various subclasses. In the paper, we focus on rewb containing a single capturing group and $k$ references. For this class, Uezato~[CPM 2026] gave an $O((k n^2 m^2)$ time and $O(n^2m^2)$ space algorithm, where $m$ is the length of the regular expression $R$ and $n$ is the length of the string $Q$. For the special case of $k=1$, Nogami and Terauchi~[MFCS 2025] gave an $O(n^2m^2)$ time and $O(n+ m^2)$ space algorithm. On the other hand, Nogami, Nakamura, and Terauchi~[arXiv 2026] gave a conditional lower bound, showing that we cannot solve the problem in $O(n^{2-ε} \mathrm{poly}(m))$ for any $ε> 0$ assuming the orthogonal vector hypothesis. Our main result is a new algorithm that runs in $O(n^2m)$ time and uses $O(nm)$ space. This improves the above results (by a factor of $km$ and $m$, respectively) and the former's space bound (by a factor of $nm$). We also show how to extend our algorithm to handle a slightly more general class of ordered and single-nested rewbs.

</details>


### [251] [High-Multiplicity Bin Packing is FPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16923)
*Tomohiro Koana,Soh Kumabe*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文解决了高多重集装箱问题在d个不同物品尺寸参数下的FPT可解性（此前为公开难题），给出了确定性O*(2^{d^{O(d)}})时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高多重集装箱（high-multiplicity bin packing）在参数d（不同物品尺寸数）下是否为固定参数可解（FPT）长期未决。Goemans与Rothvoß (JACM 2020) 仅给出XP算法，是否存在FPT算法是核心开放问题。

Method: 将装箱问题建模为整数线性规划（ILP），变量数至多(d+1)d^d。以“箱配置”（bin configuration，记录单个箱中各类型物品数量）为基础，按各坐标对d取模的余数对配置分类；每类用一个变量表示箱数、d个变量表示物品总数，并利用每类凸包的整数分解性质（integer decomposition property），保证每个可行ILP解对应一个合法装箱。

Result: 给出确定性O*(2^{d^{O(d)}})时间算法，证明了高多重集装箱问题在参数d下是FPT的，正面回答了该开放问题。

Conclusion: 通过ILP建模、按模d余数分类箱配置并借助整数分解性质，该工作将高多重集装箱的复杂度从XP提升至FPT，解决了Goemans–Rothvoß遗留的核心开放问题。

Abstract: Bin packing asks whether a collection of items can be packed into at most a given number of bins of a given capacity. We consider the high-multiplicity setting with $d$ distinct item sizes, in which both the item sizes and the number of items of each size are encoded in binary. Goemans and Rothvos (JACM 2020) gave an XP algorithm parameterized by $d$. Whether this problem is fixed-parameter tractable (FPT) in $d$ has remained a central open problem.
  We resolve this question by giving a deterministic $O^*(2^{d^{O(d)}})$-time algorithm. We formulate bin packing as an integer linear program (ILP) with at most $(d+1)d^d$ variables. A bin configuration records the number of items of each type in one bin. We partition these configurations by their coordinate remainders modulo $d$. For each class, we use one variable for the bin count and $d$ variables for the total item counts. The convex hull of each class has the integer decomposition property, which guarantees that every feasible ILP solution corresponds to a packing.

</details>


### [252] [Rank-One Matrix Discrepancy and Algorithmic Kadison--Singer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17266)
*Ekene Ezeunala,Haotian Jiang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文给出确定性多项式时间算法，对秩至多一的Hermitian矩阵求符号，使和的范数被平方和范数的常数倍控制，从而确定性解决Kadison–Singer问题（Weaver的KS₂形式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kadison–Singer问题长期是算子代数与量子信息中的核心猜想，其Weaver KS₂形式此前仅有非构造性或随机化证明，缺乏确定性多项式时间算法。

Method: 针对秩至多一的Hermitian矩阵H₁,…,H_N，设计确定性多项式时间算法求解符号s∈{±1}^N，使‖∑sᵢHᵢ‖≤13‖∑Hᵢ²‖^{1/2}；并由此推导单位分解向量情形下的划分算法。

Result: 算法保证‖∑sᵢHᵢ‖≤13‖∑Hᵢ²‖^{1/2}；对满足∑vᵢvᵢ*=I且‖vᵢ‖²≤δ的向量，得到划分[N]=S₁∪S₂，使‖∑_{i∈Sⱼ}vᵢvᵢ*−I/2‖≤(13/2)√δ（j=1,2），均为通用常数界。

Conclusion: 该工作给出Kadison–Singer问题（Weaver KS₂表述）的确定性多项式时间算法，且界为通用常数，具有构造性意义。

Abstract: We give a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm that, given rational Hermitian matrices $H_1,\dots,H_N$ of rank at most one, finds signs $s\in\{\pm1\}^N$ with $\|\sum_i s_i H_i\|\le 13\|\sum_i H_i^2\|^{1/2}$. As a corollary, for vectors $v_i$ with $\sum_i v_iv_i^*=I$ and $\|v_i\|^2\leδ$, the signs yield a partition $[N] = S_1 \cup S_2$ such that each part satisfies $\|\sum_{i \in S_j} v_i v_i^* - \frac{I}{2}\| \leq \frac{13}{2}\sqrtδ$ for $j = 1,2$. This gives a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm for the Kadison--Singer problem, in Weaver's equivalent discrepancy-theoretic $\mathsf{KS}_2$ formulation, with a universal constant.

</details>


### [253] [High Probability Streaming Lower Bounds for $F_2$ Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17286)
*William Swartworth,David P. Woodruff,Samson Zhou*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文解决了插入型流模型中 $F_2$ 估计对失败概率 $\delta$ 的最优依赖问题，证明了一个紧的高概率下界，并提出了两种在结构假设下的互补算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前 Braverman 与 Zamir [STOC 2025] 已解决常数失败概率下 $F_2$ 估计的空间复杂度，但对失败参数 $\delta$ 的最优依赖仍未确定，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明 $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-近似 $F_2$ 估计的紧下界 $\Omega\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}\,\log\frac{\varepsilon\sqrt{n}}{\log(1/\delta)}\right)$；针对噪声敏感性下现有多尺度直和论证失效的问题，引入噪声鲁棒的通信原语 "Exam Mostly Set Disjointness" 及其单向下界，并嵌入多尺度归约。此外，对频数上限为 $B$ 的流采用连续 $F_0$ 跟踪的子采样方法；对 $k$-稀疏流采用基于近似 Morris 计数器的两阶段草图。

Result: 得到正确的高概率 $\log(1/\delta)$ 依赖的紧下界；频数受限流中将 $\log(n)$ 因子替换为 $\text{polylog}(B)$；$k$-稀疏流中将 $\log n$ 替换为 $\log k$，并进一步获得对流长的 $\log\log m$ 依赖。

Conclusion: 本文完备刻画了 $F_2$ 估计对失败概率 $\delta$ 的最优依赖，并通过噪声鲁棒通信原语与多尺度归约建立了紧下界，同时给出两种结构假设下更优的互补算法。

Abstract: Estimating the second frequency moment ($F_2$) of an underlying frequency vector is a fundamental problem in the streaming model. While recent work by Braverman and Zamir [STOC 2025] resolved the space complexity for constant failure probability in the insertion-only model, the optimal dependence on the failure parameter $δ$ remained open.
  We close this gap by proving a tight high-probability lower bound of $Ω\left(\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}\log\frac{1}δ\,\log\frac{\varepsilon\sqrt{n}}{\log(1/δ)}\right)$ for $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-approximate $F_2$ estimation. The key challenge is the failure of prior multi-scale direct sum arguments under noise sensitivity. We introduce a noise-robust communication primitive, Exam Mostly Set Disjointness, and prove an $Ω\left(\frac{m}{t}\log\frac{1}δ\right)$ one-way lower bound. Embedding this into a multi-scale reduction yields the correct $\log(1/δ)$ dependence.
  We also give two complementary algorithms under natural structure assumptions. For streams with frequency bound $B$, we design a subsampling method using continuous $F_0$ tracking that replaces a $\log(n)$ factor with $\text{polylog}(B)$. For $k$-sparse streams, we develop a two-stage sketch using approximate Morris counters, replacing $\log n$ with $\log k$ and achieving a further $\log\log m$ dependence on stream length.

</details>


### [254] [Online Allocation using Few Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17343)
*Matthew Faw,Sahil Singla,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，将随机顺序（RO）模型下的近似算法转换为 p-Preview 模型（推广了 SSPI 与 p-Sample）下的算法，从而在对抗到达、采样可用的设定下，为在线资源分配、广义在线负载均衡及在线混合装填-覆盖问题取得近最优竞争比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线分配问题中 n 个请求对 m 种资源以对抗顺序到达，必须即时且不可撤销地处理，涵盖最大化价值的在线资源分配与最小化 makespan 的在线负载均衡。此前在 SSPI 与 p-Sample 等对抗顺序采样模型下，已有算法是问题特定的，且对 ε、m、n 的依赖显著更差；而随机顺序（RO）模型下已有近最优算法，因此希望建立通用归约以缩小差距。

Method: 提出 p-Preview 采样模型，统一并推广 Single-Sample Prophet Inequality（SSPI，请求从未知分布抽样、算法事先获得每个分布的一个独立样本）与 p-Sample（请求对抗、但随机 p 比例作为训练数据预先揭示）。核心是构造一个通用框架，把任意 RO 近似算法转换为 p-Preview 模型下的算法。

Result: 作为推论，在 Online Resource Allocation、广义 Online Load Balancing 以及在线混合装填-覆盖问题上，于这些对抗顺序采样模型中取得近最优界，显著改进了 [Ghuge, Singla, Wang (STOC'25)] 与 [Gupta and Molinaro (SODA'26)] 的已有结果。

Conclusion: 通过 p-Preview 模型下的通用 RO 到采样模型的归约，统一并改进了对抗顺序采样在线分配问题的算法，获得近最优的 (1±ε) 竞争比，改善了对 ε、m、n 的依赖。

Abstract: We study online allocation problems where $n$ requests over $m$ resources arrive in an adversarial order and must be served immediately and irrevocably. This framework captures both Online Resource Allocation, where the goal is to maximize value subject to resource budgets, and Online Load Balancing, where the goal is to minimize the makespan. We seek $(1\pmε)$-competitive algorithms in the large-budget or large-makespan regime.
  We consider a sampling model that generalizes the following two well-studied sampling models. In the Single-Sample Prophet Inequality ($\mathsf{SSPI}$) model, request $t$ is drawn from an unknown distribution $\mathcal{D}_t$, and the algorithm is given one independent sample from each $\mathcal{D}_t$ before the online phase. In the $p$-$\mathsf{Sample}$ model, the requests are adversarial, but a uniformly random $p$-fraction is revealed upfront as training data.
  Although near-optimal algorithms are known in the easier random-order model ($\mathsf{RO}$), where the requests arrive in a uniformly random order, prior algorithms for $\mathsf{SSPI}$ and $p$-$\mathsf{Sample}$ were problem-specific and incurred substantially worse dependencies on $ε$, $m$, and $n$. Our main contribution is a general framework that converts $\mathsf{RO}$ algorithms into algorithms for the $p$-$\mathsf{Preview}$ model, a model that generalizes both $\mathsf{SSPI}$ and $p$-$\mathsf{Sample}$. As consequences, we obtain near-optimal bounds for Online Resource Allocation, generalized Online Load Balancing, and online mixed packing-covering problems in these adversarial-order sampling models, significantly improving the bounds of [Ghuge, Singla, Wang (STOC'25)] and [Gupta and Molinaro (SODA'26)].

</details>


### [255] [The Classical Weisfeiler-Leman Algorithm Stabilizes in $O(n)$ Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17364)
*Simon Döring,Daniel Neuen*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文证明了经典Weisfeiler-Leman算法在5(n-1)次迭代后终止，改进了此前O(n log n)的上界，并与Ω(n)下界渐近匹配；同时推广到k维情形，得到O(n^{k-1}/(k-2)! + n^{k-2})的上界，且对更一般的着色序列类该上界基本紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Weisfeiler-Leman算法（2维WL）虽起源于图同构问题的启发式，但与代数图论、逻辑、证明复杂度、组合优化及机器学习等密切相关，其迭代终止次数的上界是理解算法行为与计算能力的关键；此前最优上界为O(n log n)，与已知下界Ω(n)存在较大差距。

Method: 作者针对2维Weisfeiler-Leman算法的迭代过程进行分析，证明了其终止所需的迭代次数上界为5(n-1)；在此基础上进一步论证了对于更大的k元组着色序列类，可推广得到k维WL算法的迭代次数上界。

Result: 2维情况下，迭代终止次数上界由O(n log n)改进为5(n-1)，并与Ω(n)的下界渐近匹配；对每个k≥3，k维WL算法的迭代次数上界改进为O(n^{k-1}/(k-2)! + n^{k-2})，且对更一般的着色序列类，这些上界对所有k≥3基本是紧的。

Conclusion: 该工作显著缩小了Weisfeiler-Leman算法迭代次数上界与下界之间的差距，并将结果统一推广至任意维度，对相关理论与应用领域具有基础性意义。

Abstract: The classical Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm (also known as the $2$-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm) is a simple combinatorial algorithm that was originally designed as a heuristic for the graph isomorphism problem. However, it has also numerous connections to other areas such as algebraic graph theory, logics, proof complexity, combinatorial optimization and machine learning.
  We prove that the classical Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm terminates after $5(n-1)$ iterations. This improves over the previous best upper bound of $O(n \log n)$ by Lichter, Ponomarenko and Schweitzer [LICS 2019], and asymptotically matches the known lower bound of $Ω(n)$ by Fürer [ICALP 2001].
  Additionally, building on our results for the $2$-dimensional case, we obtain an improved upper bound of $O(n^{k-1}/(k-2)! + n^{k-2})$ on the number of iterations performed by the $k$-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm, for every $k \geq 3$. Our arguments actually hold for a larger class of sequences of colorings of $k$-tuples; in this larger class our upper bounds are essentially tight for all $k \geq 3$.

</details>


### [256] [PrecPack: An Efficient Open-Source Exact Solver for Bin Packing with Generalized Precedence Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17368)
*Sunkanghong Wang,Zhengzhong Ricky You,Roberto Baldacci,Baichuan Mo,Hu Qin,Lijun Wei,Zhou Xu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出 PrecPack，一个面向带广义优先级约束的装箱问题（BPP-GP）的统一精确求解器，扩展了分支-界定-记忆（BBR）方法，支持任意非负优先级权重，并开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装箱与装配线应用中的资源高效利用需要同时考虑容量与优先级约束。BPP-GP 是强 NP 难问题，但现有精确算法主要针对经典特例，通用 BPP-GP 仅有紧凑整数模型和启发式方法，缺乏高效的开源精确求解器。

Method: 提出 PrecPack，将 BBR 扩展到任意非负优先级权重，并自然退化为经典情形；采用广义状态刻画跨箱仍然有效的约束，并通过分支、支配和冲突感知下界处理；根节点列生成使用定点算术计算数值有效的对偶界用于剪枝或证明最优性；提供通用编程与命令行接口、独立分配校验、显式终止状态和可复现批量执行，核心流程不依赖商业软件。

Result: 在同机单线程的经典装配线基准上，相较领先的开源 BBR 实现，更多实例被证明最优且平均计算时间显著缩短；与已有的带优先级装箱及 BPP-GP 基准结果比较，在多数基准集上更多实例被证明最优，报告的平均 gap 更小。

Conclusion: PrecPack 为通用 BPP-GP 提供了高效、可复用、可验证的开源精确求解方案，并在多个基准上优于现有开源实现；代码以 MIT 许可证发布。

Abstract: Efficient resource use in packing and assembly-line applications requires decisions that jointly account for capacity and precedence constraints. The strongly NP-hard bin packing problem with generalized precedence constraints (BPP-GP) models such decisions by minimizing the number of ordered, capacitated bins required to pack weighted items, even when precedence requirements span multiple bins. Existing exact algorithms primarily focus on classical special cases, whereas general BPP-GP has been addressed only via compact integer models and heuristics, with no efficient open-source exact solver. We present PrecPack, a unified exact solver that extends branch-bound-and-remember (BBR) to arbitrary nonnegative precedence weights and naturally specializes to the classical cases. Generalized states capture restrictions that remain active across future bins, which are addressed through branching, dominance, and conflict-aware lower bounds. Root column generation uses fixed-point arithmetic to compute numerically valid dual bounds for pruning or to prove optimality. To support reuse and verification, we provide common programming and command-line interfaces, independent assignment checking, explicit termination statuses, and reproducible batch execution; the core procedures require no commercial software. In same-machine, single-threaded comparisons on classic assembly-line benchmarks, more instances are proven optimal, and average computing times are substantially reduced relative to leading source-available BBR implementations. Further comparisons with published benchmark results for bin packing with precedence constraints and BPP-GP also show that more instances were proved optimal and that reported average gaps were smaller on most benchmark sets. PrecPack is released under the MIT License at https://github.com/Sunkanghong-Wang/PrecPack.

</details>


### [257] [Pseudometric-Weighted Correlation Clustering via Spectral Preclustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17403)
*Chenglin Fan,Dahoon Lee,Euiwoong Lee*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究伪度量加权关联聚类，提出随机多项式时间的 (2+ε)-近似算法，将此前最优的 10/3 近似因子改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪度量加权关联聚类中，每对顶点带有非负不一致权重且权重满足三角不等式；此前已知最优近似因子为 10/3，亟需改进。加权设定比无权情形更难，需要同时控制可行对的总权重与成对边际的加权误差。

Method: 将无权关联聚类的 cluster-LP 框架扩展到伪度量权重：1）谱预聚类在保持近优解的同时，将可行对总权重限制在 poly(1/ε)·OPT；2）聚合式 Ptolemy 型不等式给出度数乘积界与见证簇内随机游走的暖启动，使构造可使用常数长度游走；3）从有界子簇松弛中采样簇，采用带随机停止时间的相关舍入；4）利用熵界与三角不等式将加权边际误差归因于可行对而非总输入权重；5）重复采样与原子级覆盖修正得到支撑在多项式多个簇上的显式可行 cluster-LP 解。

Result: 构造出取值至多 (1+ε)·OPT 的显式可行 cluster-LP 解；重缩放 ε 后经因子 2 舍入，得到 (2+ε)-近似保证，改进此前 10/3 的近似因子。

Conclusion: 该算法将 cluster-LP 框架成功推广至伪度量加权关联聚类，通过谱预聚类、Ptolemy 不等式、相关舍入与熵界等工具实现 (2+ε)-近似，突破了原有的 10/3 壁垒。

Abstract: We study pseudometric-weighted correlation clustering, where every pair of vertices carries a nonnegative disagreement weight and the weights satisfy the triangle inequality. For every fixed $\varepsilon>0$, we give a randomized polynomial-time $(2+\varepsilon)$-approximation, improving the previously best known factor of $10/3$. Our algorithm extends the cluster-LP framework for unweighted correlation clustering to pseudometric weights. The weighted setting requires controlling both the total weight of admissible pairs and the weighted error in pairwise marginals.
  Our spectral preclustering preserves a near-optimal solution while bounding the total admissible weight by $\operatorname{poly}(1/\varepsilon)\mathrm{OPT}$, where $\mathrm{OPT}$ is the optimal clustering cost. An aggregated Ptolemy-type inequality yields a degree-product bound and a warm start for random walks within witness clusters, allowing the construction to use walks of constant length. We sample clusters from a bounded sub-cluster relaxation using correlated rounding with a randomized stopping time. An entropy bound and the triangle inequality charge the weighted marginal error to the admissible pairs rather than to the total input weight. Repeated sampling and atom-wise coverage corrections produce an explicit feasible cluster-LP solution supported on polynomially many clusters, with value at most $(1+\varepsilon)\mathrm{OPT}$. After rescaling $\varepsilon$, factor-$2$ rounding gives the stated approximation guarantee.

</details>


### [258] [Determinant maximization subject to a partition matroid constraint via stable distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17407)
*Yihang Sun,Jan Vondrak*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究在划分拟阵中选取独立集以最大化所选向量构成矩阵行列式的问题，给出多项式时间近似算法，达到 e^{-O(d)} 的近似比，并推广至秩 m≤d 的 m 维体积近似（因子 e^{O(m)}）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有算法仅能估计最优值而无法给出对应解，本文旨在设计能实际构造解的多项式时间近似算法，并匹配已有估计算法在指数常数意义下的近似保证。

Method: 基于 Nikolov 和 Singh 提出的鞍点松弛，并引入基于 1/2-稳定分布的随机变换，将鞍点松弛转化为更易处理的多线性松弛。

Result: 算法可在多项式时间内找到集合 I，使得 det(∑_{i∈I} v_i v_i^T) ≥ e^{-O(d)} OPT；对秩 m≤d 的划分拟阵，可在 e^{O(m)} 因子内近似所选向量张成的 m 维体积。

Conclusion: 该算法首次在划分拟阵约束下实现行列式最大化问题的可构造解近似，近似比与已有仅估计最优值的算法在指数常数上一致，核心创新是 1/2-稳定分布随机变换结合鞍点松弛。

Abstract: Given vectors $v_i \in {\mathbb R}^d$, we consider the problem of choosing a set $I$ independent in a partition matroid in order to maximize the determinant $\det (\sum_{i \in I} v_i v_i^T)$. Our main result is a polynomial-time approximation algorithm that finds a solution of value $det ( \sum_{i \in I} v_{i} v_{i}^T) \geq e^{-O(d)} OPT$, where $OPT = \max_{I^*} det ( \sum_{i \in I^*} v_{i} v_{i}^T)$. For partition matroids of rank $m \leq d$, we give a similar result for approximating the $m$-dimensional volume spanned by the chosen vectors, within a factor of $e^{O(m)}$.
  This matches earlier known algorithms that estimate the optimal value but do not find the corresponding solution, up to a constant in the exponent. Similar to these estimation algorithms, our algorithm is based on the saddle-point relaxation proposed by Nikolov and Singh. A new ingredient is a randomized transformation based on $1/2$-stable distributions, which converts the saddle-point relaxation into a more convenient multilinear relaxation.

</details>


### [259] [Stuffed IBLTs: Optimal Linear Multiset Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17487)
*Jonas Klausen,Rasmus Pagh,Stefan Walzer*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出 Stuffed IBLT——一种用于精确恢复整数向量 v 的线性草图。当 ||v||₀ ≤ n 且 ||v||∞ ≤ L 时，它能高概率恢复 v，支持任意更新下的常数时间维护与 O(n) 时间解码，空间使用接近信息论最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具备类似功能（如 IBLT）的构造在空间、时间与错误概率的权衡上不够理想，尚无法同时逼近信息论最优空间并保持常数时间更新与线性时间解码。

Method: 将 Walzer 的空间耦合技术（SODA '21）、Houen、Pagh 与 Walzer 的纯度启发式（SOSA '23）以及 backyarding 技术（ESA '24; ICALP '24）进行细致组合，消除以往方法的瓶颈。

Result: 对任意正常数 c、ε，当 n 足够大且 u ≥ n^{1+Ω(1)} 时，Stuffed IBLT 的空间使用在信息论最优的 (1+ε) 倍以内，更新为常数时间，解码时间为 O(n)，失败概率为 n^{−c}。同时给出下界，证明对 c 的依赖在 peeling-based 方法类中最优。

Conclusion: Stuffed IBLT 在空间/时间/错误概率权衡上全面优于包括 IBLT 在内的所有先前构造，其性能在 c、ε 依赖之外基本达到理论最优，适用于流式算法与（多）集合调和问题。

Abstract: A \emph{linear sketch} is a randomized linear mapping of a vector $v$ to a lower dimensional sketch vector, designed to preserve relevant information about $v$. We consider sketches of vectors $v \in Z^u$ (for $u \in N$), designed for exact recovery of $v$ from its sketch. Concretely, our \emph{Stuffed IBLT} is a linear sketch configured with a capacity $n \in N$ and a multiplicity limit $L \in N$ and will recover $v$ with high probability whenever $||v||_0 \leq n$ and $||v||_\infty \leq L$. The sketch can be maintained efficiently under unrestricted updates to $v$, i.e., $v$ is not subject to any constraints in between decoding requests. This makes the sketch useful for streaming algorithms and for solving the (multi)set reconciliation problem.
  For any positive constants $c$, $ε$, and for large enough $n$ and $u \geq n^{1+Ω(1)}$, the space usage of a Stuffed IBLT is within a factor $1+ε$ from the information-theoretic optimum while allowing updates in constant time, and decoding in time $O(n)$ with failure probability $n^{-c}$. This improves the space/time/error probability trade-off over all prior constructions with similar functionality, including the Invertible Bloom Lookup Table (IBLT). The performance of the Stuffed IBLT is essentially the best we could hope for, up to the dependence on $c$ and $ε$. We make the dependence on these parameters explicit, and further show a lower bound demonstrating that the dependence on $c$ is optimal within the class of peeling-based approaches. Our improvement comes from a careful combination of Walzer's spatial coupling technique (SODA '21), the purity heuristic of Houen, Pagh, and Walzer (SOSA '23), and backyarding (Belazzougui, Kucherov, and Walzer, ESA '24; Fleischhacker, Green Larsen, Obremski, and Simkin, ICALP '24), allowing us to eliminate bottlenecks of past approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Joint UAV Activation and Placement for Post-Disaster Wireless Restoration via a Hybrid Quantum-Inspired Evolutionary Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16019)
*Fatima Azzahraa Amarcha,Lahcen Hassine,Rachid Saadane,Mohamed Rahouti,Rachid Ahl Laamara,Abdallah Slaoui,Hany S. khalifa*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出HKQEA混合算法求解灾后无人机空中基站的联合激活与连续空间部署问题，在满足覆盖与最小间距约束下最小化无人机数量，最佳可行解为8架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后地面通信基础设施失效，需快速部署无人机作为空中基站恢复无线连接；在连续空间中同时决定无人机激活数量与位置，并兼顾覆盖与最小间距约束，是一个受约束的资源高效部署难题。

Method: 提出混合K-means量子启发式进化算法（HKQEA），融合K-means引导初始化、校准型惩罚可行性目标、非精英进化搜索与量子启发式学习更新。基于50次独立运行进行实验，并与NSGA-II、PSO及HKQEA的精英变体对比，另附采购级成本分析。

Result: 50次独立运行中HKQEA取得的最佳全可行解为8架无人机；平均覆盖率为98.94%、非重叠率为99.94%、最小距离满足率为99.68%。对比表明其在探索、收敛行为与可行性保持之间取得更优平衡；成本分析显示机队由10架减至8架可降低20%硬件数量，对应25%的简化节省比例。

Conclusion: 该框架在受限部署问题中能可靠保持可行性并实现资源高效配置，展示了其在灾后通信恢复中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: In post-disaster environments, the failure of terrestrial communication infrastructure necessitates the rapid deployment of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) as aerial base stations to restore wireless connectivity. This paper addresses the joint UAV activation-and-placement problem in continuous space, with the objective of minimizing the number of deployed UAVs while satisfying coverage and minimum-separation constraints. To solve this problem, we propose a Hybrid K-means Quantum-Inspired Evolutionary Algorithm (HKQEA) that combines K-means-guided initialization, a calibrated penalty-based feasibility objective, non-elitist evolutionary search, and a quantum-inspired learning update. Experimental results over 50 independent runs show that HKQEA attains a best fully feasible solution with 8 UAVs, while achieving average values of 98.94\% for coverage, 99.94\% for non-overlap, and 99.68\% for minimum-distance satisfaction. Comparative evaluation against standard Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II (NSGA-II), Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm (PSO) and an elitist variant of HKQEA further shows that the proposed method provides a more favorable balance among exploration, convergence behavior, and reliable feasibility preservation in constrained deployment problems. An illustrative procurement-level cost analysis also indicates that reducing the fleet from 10 UAVs to 8 can yield a 20\% reduction in hardware count, corresponding to a simplified savings ratio of 25\% for the studied deployment setting. These results demonstrate the potential of the proposed framework for resource-efficient post-disaster communication restoration.

</details>


### [261] [Scaled Hippocampus-inspired Neural Networks on Neuromorphic Memristive Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16429)
*Joseph A. Kilgore,Jeffrey D. Kopsick,Zahin Ahmed,Giorgio A. Ascoli,Gina C. Adam*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文受啮齿动物CA3海马亚区启发，提出首个在忆阻器硬件上实现、具有神经元多样性与生物真实静息态动力学的脉冲神经网络，并通过网络降尺度方法在小规模网络上复现大规模网络动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海马体具有丰富的结构多样性、稀疏通信和高效鲁棒的动力学特性，为新型计算能力提供启发，尤其适合与新兴硬件技术协同设计。作者希望将生物真实算法与忆阻器硬件结合，探索类脑计算潜力。

Method: 从啮齿动物CA3海马亚区获取灵感，构建具有神经元多样性和生物真实静息态动力学的脉冲神经网络；提出基于四目标函数的网络降尺度方法，得到含179个Izhikevich模型神经元、3种神经元类型和17,996个突触的小规模CA3网络；采用贪婪算法将其映射到FPGA/忆阻器平台，使用18,316个忆阻器。

Result: 小规模网络在静息态动力学上与数量级更大的全规模网络相似；硬件实现受益于忆阻器噪声，表现出连续周期性行为，性能优于仿真硬件。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了生物真实算法在新兴硬件上用于神经形态计算的潜力。

Abstract: The hippocampus, a key brain region for learning and memory, exhibits rich structural diversity, sparse communication, and robust dynamics with incredible energy efficiency. It offers promising insights for novel computing capabilities, particularly when co-designed with emerging hardware technologies. In this work, we draw inspiration from the rodent CA3 hippocampal subregion to develop the first spiking neural network with neuronal diversity and biologically-realistic resting state dynamics demonstrated on memristor hardware. We propose a network downscaling methodology utilizing a 4-prong objective function and demonstrate a small-scale CA3-inspired network with 179 Izhikevich-modeled neurons, 3 neuronal types and 17,996 synapses with similar resting-state dynamics as the orders-of-magnitude larger full-scale network. The small-scale network is mapped to an FPGA/memristor platform using a greedy algorithm and 18,316 memristors. Benefiting from memristor noise, the hardware implementation shows continuous periodic behavior, outperforming simulated hardware. This work showcases the potential of biologically-realistic algorithms on emerging hardware for neuromorphic computing.

</details>


### [262] [LLMDE: A Large Language Model-Driven Differential Evolution Algorithm for Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16846)
*Rong Chai,Vaclav Snasel,Xiaopeng Wang,Seyedali Mirjalili,Crina Grosan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出大语言模型驱动的差分进化算法 LLMDE，通过提示工程让 LLM 动态选择变异策略并配置控制参数，在 CEC2022 基准与 CVaR 投资组合优化任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统差分进化算法依赖人工设计的超参数与变异策略，调参成本高且泛化性受限，因此需要降低对手工超参数设计的依赖。

Method: 提出 LLMDE：采用提示工程策略，将优化反馈作为输入，引导大语言模型动态选择变异策略并配置控制参数，从而增强 DE 性能。在 CEC2022 基准上与标准 DE 及代表性元启发式算法对比；并先用因子分析与 K-means 聚类进行选股，再将 LLMDE 用于求解带预算和最低期望收益约束的 CVaR 投资组合优化问题。

Result: LLMDE 在 CEC2022 基准上取得有竞争力的性能，并在复杂约束优化任务中持续生成高质量解。

Conclusion: 结果验证了将 LLM 嵌入元启发式算法的可行性，为 LLM 辅助的先进优化技术设计提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: This study proposes a Large Language Model-Driven Differential Evolution (LLMDE) algorithm to reduce the reliance on handcrafted hyperparameter design. The proposed algorithm leverages a prompt engineering strategy, allowing large language models (LLMs) to dynamically select mutation strategies and configure control parameters guided by optimization feedback, thus enhancing the performance of the DE algorithm. We evaluate the performance of LLMDE on the CEC2022 benchmark suite, comparing it with standard DE and representative metaheuristics. Furthermore, we employ factor analysis and K-means clustering for stock selection, and then apply LLMDE to solve the Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) portfolio optimization problem using the selected stocks, subject to budget and minimum expected return constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMDE achieves competitive performance on the benchmark suite while continuously generating high-quality solutions for complex constrained optimization tasks. These outcomes successfully demonstrate the viability of embedding LLMs within metaheuristics, paving a promising path toward the design of advanced LLM-assisted optimization techniques.

</details>


### [263] [Bio-Inspired Palette Evolution in Indirectly Encoded Substrates: Timescale Compatibility Shapes Activation Function Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17067)
*Romain Claret,Michael O'Neill,Paul Cotofrei,Kilian Stoffel*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文把"进化过程中应使用哪些激活函数"建模为元学习问题，设计了13种激活函数集合（palette）动态调控策略，其中11种受生物适应机制启发。在3000+次实验中，生物启发策略在求解率上与调优基线相当，但收敛速度最高快2倍；策略排名随问题类型反转，且成败主要由"策略时间尺度与评估窗口是否匹配"决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接编码神经网络可为每个节点分配不同激活函数，但合适函数难以预知：仅含标准单调函数时奇偶（parity）等问题不可解，而全包含的palette又不如精选palette。因此需要解决"进化如何发现该用哪些函数"这一问题。

Method: 将该问题视为元学习，设计13种在进化中修改可用激活函数集合的策略（11种受生物适应机制启发，加基线与oracle对照）。每种策略把一条生物学原理转化为进化算子，如昼夜节律启发的振荡门控按固定日程让函数进出palette，免疫启发的克隆选择则永久保护与适应度持续相关的函数。在parity与非parity问题上开展3000+次运行：先仅进化激活palette，再在更难问题上共同进化每节点聚合palette；并用新随机种子做独立复现。

Result: 独立复现确认存在稳定的高可靠性层级，Circadian居首。生物启发策略达到调优基线的求解率，但收敛快至2倍，Circadian使总计算量减半。策略排名在不同问题类型间发生反转，无单一策略在全部领域占优。策略成败主要取决于时间尺度兼容性：特征时间尺度与进化评估窗口匹配者显著优于过慢者。重标定最慢策略可绕过振荡障碍：九种解均以非振荡激活配合min或max聚合解决parity。

Conclusion: 实践指导原则是：让机制的时间尺度与评估预算相匹配；据此重标定过慢策略即可突破振荡瓶颈。

Abstract: Indirectly encoded neural networks can assign different activation functions to individual nodes, but the right functions are rarely known in advance. When the available set contains only standard monotonic functions, problems like parity become unsolvable, yet an all-inclusive palette underperforms a curated one. How should evolution discover which functions to use? We address this as a meta-learning problem, designing 13 strategies (11 inspired by biological adaptation mechanisms, plus baseline and oracle controls) that modify the set of available activation functions during evolution. Each strategy translates a biological principle into an evolutionary operator: for example, circadian-inspired oscillatory gating cycles functions in and out of the palette on a fixed schedule, while immune-inspired Clonal Selection permanently protects functions that consistently correlate with fitness. We evaluate all strategies across more than 3,000 runs on parity and non-parity problems, first evolving the activation palette alone, then co-evolving a per-node aggregation palette on harder problems; an independent replication with new seeds confirms a stable high-reliability tier, with Circadian holding its top rank. Bio-inspired strategies match the solve rate of a tuned baseline but converge up to twice as fast, with Circadian halving total compute. Strategy rankings reverse across problem types, with no strategy dominating all domains. Strategy success is largely shaped by timescale compatibility: strategies whose characteristic timescale matches the evolutionary evaluation window consistently outperform those that operate too slowly. The practical guideline: match the mechanism's timescale to the evaluation budget. Rescaling the slowest strategy bypasses the oscillatory barrier entirely: all nine solutions solve parity with non-oscillatory activations paired with min or max aggregation.

</details>


### [264] [Machine Zygote: Causal Biparental Heredity Before Learning in a Germline--Soma Artificial Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17300)
*Lyes Saad Saoud*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本研究提出"Machine Zygote"计算架构（种系-体细胞分离），通过预注册的4×4双列杂交实验（640个后代）与匹配背景干预（n=60），在无学习的仿真新生儿智能体中验证了因果性双亲遗传的存在，但否定了"递归发育动力学是必要条件"这一更强假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工个体发生、发育编码、机器人繁殖与可继承控制器已是成熟方向，但一个更窄的问题尚未解决：新生儿人工智能体能否在学习之前表现出可测量的双亲遗传？这种依赖能否被因果性地分离，而非仅从亲子相似性推断？作者希望建立以干预为核心的框架，将遗传、发育、随机变异与出生后学习区分开来。

Method: 提出"Machine Zygote"种系-体细胞架构：两条亲代种系独立突变并重组为合子，合子参数化一个初始通用的八模块体细胞的发育；体细胞随后被冻结并在无学习条件下评估。设计预注册的4×4双列杂交（diallel）共640个后代，检验母本（dam）与父本（sire）效应；并开展匹配背景干预（n=60），在固定重组与随机背景的条件下替换单一亲代种系，与同亲本再突变对照比较；另设准静态无动力学消融实验检验发育依赖假设。

Result: 双列杂交显示，经Holm校正后六个行为性状中有五个存在显著的母本与父本依赖，五个主要性状中亲本及交互成分占建模方差的36%–53%。在匹配背景干预中，替换单条亲代种系（保持重组与随机背景不变）导致表型变化超过同亲本再突变对照，两条亲本通道在六个性状中各有五个成立。重组在速度和步态频率上产生超亲（transgressive）后代过多。预注册的发育依赖假设未获支持：准静态无动力学消融保留了平均表型分布，但改变了亲本方差结构。

Conclusion: 该仿真支持学习前的因果性双亲遗传，但不支持"递归发育动力学是必要条件"这一更强断言；研究不主张物理遗传、生物遗传学或自主演化。其贡献在于提出以干预为中心的框架与可复现基准，用于分离遗传、发育、随机变异与出生后学习。

Abstract: Artificial ontogeny, developmental encodings, robot reproduction, and inherited controllers are established research directions, yet a narrower question remains: can a newborn artificial agent exhibit measurable biparental heredity before learning, and can that dependence be isolated causally rather than inferred only from parent-offspring resemblance? We introduce Machine Zygote, a computational germline-soma architecture designed to test this question. Two parental germlines are independently mutated and recombined into a zygote that parameterizes development of an initially generic eight-module soma, which is then frozen and evaluated without learning. A preregistered 4 x 4 diallel of 640 offspring shows significant dam and sire dependence for five of six behavioral traits after Holm correction, with parental and interaction components accounting for 36-53 percent of modeled variance across five principal traits. In matched-background interventions (n=60), substituting one parental germline while holding recombination and stochastic background fixed causes phenotype shifts exceeding a same-parent re-mutation control for five of six traits for both parental channels. Recombination also yields excess transgressive offspring for speed and gait frequency. A preregistered developmental-dependence hypothesis is not supported: a quasistatic no-dynamics ablation preserves the mean phenotype distribution while altering parental variance structure. Thus the study supports causal biparental pre-learning heredity in this simulation, but not the stronger claim that recurrent developmental dynamics are necessary. It does not establish physical heredity, biological genetics, or autonomous evolution. The contribution is an intervention-centered framework and reproducible benchmark for separating heredity, development, stochastic variation, and post-birth learning.

</details>


### [265] [A Spatiotemporal Extension of the Neuromorphic DBSCAN Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17357)
*Charles P. Rizzo,James S. Plank*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文在既有神经形态DBSCAN（"flat"与"systolic"两种结构）基础上，提出面向事件传感器数据的时空扩展结构，并讨论受限硬件下的分段/部分实现，全部实现开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件传感器数据本身具有时空特性，而此前实现的DBSCAN结构未充分挖掘这一特性；同时硬件资源受限场景需要更灵活的面积-吞吐权衡方案。

Method: 对原有"flat"（以吞吐优先）和"systolic"（以时间换空间、硬件友好但吞吐较低）两种神经形态DBSCAN结构进行时空扩展，并给出部分或分段的实现方式，在资源受限时进一步以时间换空间。所有网络结构以开源形式提供。

Result: 提出了两种结构的时空扩展版本及分段实现方案；具体性能数据摘要中未给出。

Conclusion: 时空扩展能更好利用事件数据的时空特性，分段实现在硬件受限时提供额外面积-吞吐折中，且实现全部开源。

Abstract: DBSCAN is an algorithm that denoises and clusters data. In prior work, we implemented the DBSCAN algorithm neuromorphically, introducing two constructions termed ``flat'' and ``systolic''. The ``flat'' construction prioritizes throughput, while the ``systolic'' construction trades time for space resulting in a smaller, more hardware-friendly architecture at the cost of throughput. In this work, we offer spatiotemporal extensions of these two constructions to better leverage the spatiotemporal nature of event sensor data. Moreover, as in our prior work, we discuss partial or segmented implementations that further leverage time for space when hardware resources are constrained. All network constructions are provided as open-source implementations.

</details>
